```py
<script src="raven-lite.js"></script>
<script>
const CFG = __CFG__;
const CASES = __CASES__;
const SAMPLES = __SAMPLES__;
const SUMMARY = __SUMMARY__;
RavenLite.init(CFG);

const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const CAT_CN = CFG.subtype_cn;
const BADGE = {block:'b-block', review:'b-review', watch:'b-watch', allow:'b-allow'};

function fmtPct(v){ return (v*100).toFixed(1) + '%'; }

/* ---------- 顶部指标 ---------- */
const stats = [
  [fmtPct(SUMMARY.clean), '干净集 F1'],
  [fmtPct(SUMMARY.adv), '对抗集 F1'],
  [fmtPct(SUMMARY.pervasive), '逐字符分隔集 F1'],
  [SUMMARY.auc.toFixed(3), '对抗集 AUC'],
  [fmtPct(SUMMARY.block_precision), '自动拦截档精确率'],
  [SUMMARY.latency.toFixed(2)+'ms', '单条推理耗时']
];
$('strip').innerHTML = stats.map(([v,l]) =>
  `<div class="stat"><b>${v}</b><span>${l}</span></div>`).join('');
$('foot').innerHTML =
  `阈值 ${SUMMARY.threshold} · 惩罚性误伤（自动拦截档）${fmtPct(1-SUMMARY.block_precision)} · ` +
  `图像侧 AUC ${SUMMARY.image_auc} / F1 ${SUMMARY.image_f1} · ` +
  `全部指标由 <span class="mono">eval/run_eval.py</span> 在留出模板的测试集上跑出，页面不做任何人为修饰。`;

/* ---------- 字符级 diff ---------- */
function diffHtml(before, after){
  // 极简 LCS：只为可视化，长度短，O(nm) 可接受
  const a = Array.from(before), b = Array.from(after);
  const m = a.length, n = b.length;
  const dp = Array.from({length:m+1}, () => new Uint16Array(n+1));
  for (let i=m-1;i>=0;i--) for (let j=n-1;j>=0;j--)
    dp[i][j] = a[i]===b[j] ? dp[i+1][j+1]+1 : Math.max(dp[i+1][j], dp[i][j+1]);
  let i=0,j=0,out='';
  while(i<m && j<n){
    if(a[i]===b[j]){ out+=esc(a[i]); i++; j++; }
    else if(dp[i+1][j] >= dp[i][j+1]){ out+='<del>'+esc(a[i])+'</del>'; i++; }
    else { out+='<ins>'+esc(b[j])+'</ins>'; j++; }
  }
  while(i<m){ out+='<del>'+esc(a[i++])+'</del>'; }
  while(j<n){ out+='<ins>'+esc(b[j++])+'</ins>'; }
  return out;
}
function esc(s){ return s.replace(/[&<>]/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;'}[c])); }

/* ---------- 文本检测 ---------- */
function render(text){
  if(!text.trim()){ return; }
  const r = RavenLite.score(text);
  const p = r.p;
  $('score').textContent = p.toFixed(3);
  const badge = $('badge');
  badge.textContent = r.action_cn;
  badge.className = 'badge ' + BADGE[r.action];
  const col = p >= CFG.decision_thresholds.block ? 'var(--danger)'
            : p >= CFG.decision_thresholds.review ? 'var(--warn)'
            : p >= CFG.decision_thresholds.watch ? '#667085' : 'var(--ok)';
  $('bar').style.width = Math.max(2, p*100) + '%';
  $('bar').style.background = col;
  $('meta').textContent = `阈值 ${CFG.threshold} · 处置阈值 拦截 ${CFG.decision_thresholds.block} / 复核 ${CFG.decision_thresholds.review} / 观察 ${CFG.decision_thresholds.watch}`;
  $('evn').textContent = r.hits.length + ' 项';
  $('cat').textContent = CAT_CN[r.subtype] || r.subtype;
  $('rule').textContent = r.p_rule.toFixed(3);
  $('hits').innerHTML =
    r.hits.slice(0,8).map(h => `<span class="hit">${esc(h.term)} ×${h.count}</span>`).join('') +
    (r.neg > 0 ? `<div style="margin-top:8px" class="meta">否定语境信号 ${r.neg.toFixed(1)}：`
      + r.negHits.slice(0,4).map(h=>`<span class="hit neg">${esc(h.term)}</span>`).join('')
      + ` —— 更可能是在举报/提醒，而不是在卖</div>` : '');

  const steps = r.trace.length
    ? r.trace.map(s => `<div class="step"><span class="nm">${s.step}</span>
        <span class="mono">${diffHtml(s.before, s.after)}</span></div>`).join('')
    : '<div class="meta">原文已是规范形，归一化层没有做任何改动。</div>';
  $('steps').innerHTML = steps;
}

$('input').addEventListener('input', e => render(e.target.value));
$('chips').innerHTML = SAMPLES.map(([name, txt]) =>
  `<span class="chip" data-t="${esc(txt)}">${name}</span>`).join('');
$('chips').addEventListener('click', e => {
  if(!e.target.dataset.t) return;
  $('input').value = e.target.dataset.t;
  render(e.target.dataset.t);
});
$('input').value = SAMPLES[0][1];
render(SAMPLES[0][1]);

/* ---------- 图像 ---------- */
$('imgrow').innerHTML = CASES.map((c, i) => {
  const cells = c.heat.map(row => row.map(v =>
    `<span style="background:rgba(217,45,32,${(v*0.75).toFixed(2)})"></span>`).join('')).join('');
  const col = c.p >= 0.5 ? 'var(--danger)' : 'var(--ok)';
  return `<div class="icase" title="${c.name}">
    <div class="ihead"><span>${c.kind}</span><b style="color:${col}">${c.p.toFixed(2)}</b></div>
    <div class="ibody"><img src="${c.img}" alt=""><div class="heat">${cells}</div></div>
    <div class="ihead" style="color:var(--muted);font-size:11.5px">${
      c.top_feats.map(([k,v]) => k+'='+v).join(' · ')}</div>
  </div>`;
}).join('');
</script>
</body>
</html>
"""


def main():
    bundle = load_engine()
    with open(os.path.join(ART, "results", "metrics.json"), encoding="utf-8") as f:
        metrics = json.load(f)
    cases = image_cases(bundle)
    html = build_html(bundle, cases, metrics)

    # 把推理内核内联进页面，做成真正的单文件。
    # 原来用 <script src="raven-lite.js">，只要把 index.html 单独发出去（微信、邮件、
    # 面试时拷到别人电脑）就会白屏，而报错只在控制台——演示时最尴尬的一种失败。
    with open(os.path.join(DEMO, "raven-lite.js"), encoding="utf-8") as f:
        kernel = f.read()
    assert "<script" not in kernel, "内核里出现 script 标签会破坏内联"
    assert '<script src="raven-lite.js"></script>' in html
    html = html.replace('<script src="raven-lite.js"></script>',
                        f"<script>\n{kernel}\n</script>")

    out = os.path.join(DEMO, "index.html")
    with open(out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(html)
    print(f"[ok] demo -> {out}  ({len(html)/1024:.1f} KB, 图像案例 {len(cases)} 个, 单文件自包含)")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`tools/check_metrics.py`:

```py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""指标回归闸门（metric gate）。

为什么需要它：这个项目里所有结论都是数字，README 上的 F1、报告里的消融矩阵、
简历上的每一行，全部来自 `artifacts/results/metrics.json`。如果某次改动让某个
指标悄悄变了 0.02，报告和 README 就成了假的——而且不会有任何报错。

这个脚本把**质量类指标**钉成基线，跑完 `run_all.py` 后逐一比对，超出容差即非零退出。
CI 里跑它，等于给"我说过的数字"上了一道回归测试。

刻意**不**校验的字段（它们本来就不该稳定，钉住只会制造假警报）：
    generated_at    时间戳
    *latency_ms*    单条推理耗时，依赖机器负载
    *train_seconds* 训练耗时，同上

用法：
    python tools/check_metrics.py                 # 比对，默认容差 1e-3
    python tools/check_metrics.py --tol 5e-3      # 跨平台/跨环境时放宽（CI 用这个）
    python tools/check_metrics.py --update        # 把当前结果写回基线（确认是有意改动后再用）

关于容差：模型训练里有 `x @ w`，走 BLAS。同一平台逐位可复现，但不同 OS 的
OpenBLAS 在浮点累加顺序上可能有 1e-7 级差异，经 SGD 迭代可能翻转极个别样本。
所以容差不是"随便给的宽松度"，而是"这台机器与基线机器的环境差异预算"。
"""
import argparse
import json
import os
import sys

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
METRICS = os.path.join(ROOT, "artifacts", "results", "metrics.json")
BASELINE = os.path.join(ROOT, "artifacts", "results", "baseline.json")

# 基线：字段路径 → 期望值
# 路径用 "/" 分隔，列表用下标。末尾的 [0] 表示 configs 里最后一项（最终配置）。
BASELINE_SPEC = {
    # ---- 数据规模（造数是确定性的，规模不该变）----
    "dataset/train/n": 660,
    "dataset/test_clean/n": 270,
    "dataset/test_adv/n": 270,
    "dataset/test_pervasive/n": 270,
    "dataset/test_holdout/n": 270,

    # ---- 最终配置（⑤ 混合模型）在四套测试集上的表现 ----
    "text/threshold": 0.38,
    "text/configs/[-1]/test_clean/f1": 0.9431,
    "text/configs/[-1]/test_clean/precision": 0.9206,
    "text/configs/[-1]/test_clean/recall": 0.9667,
    "text/configs/[-1]/test_adv/f1": 0.9297,
    "text/configs/[-1]/test_adv/precision": 0.9053,
    "text/configs/[-1]/test_adv/recall": 0.9556,
    "text/configs/[-1]/test_pervasive/f1": 0.9505,
    "text/configs/[-1]/test_pervasive/precision": 0.9402,
    "text/configs/[-1]/test_pervasive/recall": 0.9611,
    "text/configs/[-1]/test_holdout/f1": 0.9456,
    "text/configs/[-1]/test_holdout/precision": 0.9763,
    "text/configs/[-1]/test_holdout/recall": 0.9167,

    # ---- 对抗能力的关键证据：规则层 vs 混合模型 ----
    # 逐字符分隔集上，规则层 F1 = 0（一条都检不出）
    "text/configs/[0]/test_pervasive/f1": 0.0,
    # 归一化让规则层从 0 回到 0.889（即 0.8889）
    "text/configs/[1]/test_pervasive/f1": 0.8889,

    # ---- 分档处置 ----
    "text/tier_table/[0]/n": 151,          # block 档条数
    "text/tier_table/[0]/precision": 1.0,  # 拦截精确率 100%
    "text/tier_table/[0]/share_of_risk": 0.8389,
    "text/tier_table/[1]/share_of_risk": 0.0611,

    # ---- 账号级聚合 ----
    "account/f1": 0.9512,
    "account/precision": 0.9435,
    "account/recall": 0.959,
    "account/accounts": 163,

    # ---- 图像侧 ----
    "image/metrics/auc": 0.9734,
    "image/metrics/f1": 0.8632,
    "image/metrics/localization/mean_hit": 0.3042,
}


def resolve(obj, path):
    """按 'a/b/[0]/c' 取嵌套值，支持 [-1] 取末项。"""
    cur = obj
    for seg in path.split("/"):
        if seg.startswith("[") and seg.endswith("]"):
            idx = int(seg[1:-1])
            cur = cur[idx]
        else:
            cur = cur[seg]
    return cur


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--tol", type=float, default=1e-3, help="允许的绝对误差（默认 1e-3）")
    ap.add_argument("--update", action="store_true", help="把当前结果写成新基线")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    if not os.path.exists(METRICS):
        print(f"[失败] 找不到 {METRICS}，请先跑 python run_all.py")
        return 2

    with open(METRICS, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        m = json.load(f)

    # --update：从当前 metrics.json 重新采样一份基线快照
    if args.update:
        snap = {}
        for path in BASELINE_SPEC:
            try:
                snap[path] = resolve(m, path)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[跳过] {path} → {e}")
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(BASELINE), exist_ok=True)
        with open(BASELINE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(snap, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        print(f"基线已更新：{BASELINE}（{len(snap)} 项）")
        print("注意：确认这是有意的改动，再提交这个文件。")
        return 0

    rows, bad = [], []
    for path, expect in BASELINE_SPEC.items():
        try:
            got = resolve(m, path)
        except Exception as e:
            rows.append((path, expect, f"取不到 ({type(e).__name__})", "缺失"))
            bad.append(path)
            continue
        if isinstance(got, (int, float)) and isinstance(expect, (int, float)):
            diff = abs(float(got) - float(expect))
            ok = diff <= args.tol
            rows.append((path, expect, got, "OK" if ok else f"漂移 {diff:.6f}"))
            if not ok:
                bad.append(path)
        else:
            ok = got == expect
            rows.append((path, expect, got, "OK" if ok else "不一致"))
            if not ok:
                bad.append(path)

    w = max(len(r[0]) for r in rows)
    print(f"指标回归比对  容差 {args.tol}  共 {len(rows)} 项")
    print("-" * (w + 40))
    for path, expect, got, status in rows:
        mark = "  " if status == "OK" else "✗ "
        print(f"{mark}{path:<{w}}  期望 {expect!s:<10} 实测 {got!s:<12} {status}")
    print("-" * (w + 40))

    if bad:
        print(f"[失败] {len(bad)} 项漂移：")
        for p in bad:
            print(f"   - {p}")
        print("\n如果是无意的，回去查改动；如果是有意的（换了特征 / 改了数据），")
        print("先跑 --update 记下新基线，再同步改 README 与 docs/EVAL.md 里的数字。")
        return 1

    print(f"[通过] {len(rows)} 项全部在容差内，质量指标可复现。")
    print("（耗时类字段刻意不校验：它们依赖机器负载，见 docs/EVAL.md）")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

```

`tools/dom_smoke_test.js`:

```js
// demo 冒烟测试：用一个极简 DOM 桩跑一遍页面脚本，确认初始化与渲染不抛异常。
// 跑法：node tools/dom_smoke_test.js
//
// 注意：这里**按页面里的顺序依次执行所有 <script> 块**，不再单独 load raven-lite.js。
// 因为内核已经内联进 index.html 了，这个测试顺带就在验证"单文件自包含"这件事成立——
// 一旦有人把内核改回外部引用，这里会因为找不到 RavenLite 直接失败。
//
// 用 vm.runInThisContext 而不是 eval：页面脚本里的 const/let 是词法声明，
// eval 会给它们一个独立作用域，外面的断言根本看不见 CFG，会误判成"没注入"。
const fs = require('fs'), path = require('path');
const assert = require('assert');
const vm = require('vm');

const store = {};
function el(id) {
  if (store[id]) return store[id];
  const o = {
    id, _html: '', _text: '', style: {}, dataset: {}, value: '', className: '',
    set innerHTML(v) { this._html = v; }, get innerHTML() { return this._html; },
    set textContent(v) { this._text = v; }, get textContent() { return this._text; },
    addEventListener() {},
  };
  store[id] = o; return o;
}
global.document = { getElementById: el };
global.window = global;
global.TextEncoder = TextEncoder;

const html = fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, '..', 'demo', 'index.html'), 'utf8');
const blocks = [...html.matchAll(/<script>([\s\S]*?)<\/script>/g)].map(m => m[1]);
assert(blocks.length >= 2, `期望至少 2 个内联 script（内核 + 主逻辑），实际 ${blocks.length}`);

for (const code of blocks) vm.runInThisContext(code);

// 在同一个全局词法环境里取结果
const probe = vm.runInThisContext(`(function(){
  const g = document.getElementById.bind(document);
  const strip = g('strip').innerHTML, steps = g('steps').innerHTML, imgrow = g('imgrow').innerHTML;
  return {
    hasKernel: typeof RavenLite !== 'undefined',
    dim: (typeof CFG !== 'undefined' && CFG && CFG.dim) || 0,
    score: g('score').textContent,
    badge: g('badge').textContent,
    stats: (strip.match(/class="stat"/g) || []).length,
    steps: (steps.match(/class="step"/g) || []).length,
    hits: g('hits').innerHTML.replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '').trim(),
    imgs: (imgrow.match(/class="icase"/g) || []).length,
  };
})()`);

assert(probe.hasKernel, '内核未加载：index.html 可能又变回外部引用 raven-lite.js 了');
assert(probe.dim > 0, 'CFG 未注入');
assert(probe.score && probe.score.length, 'score 未渲染');
assert(probe.stats >= 5, `指标卡数量异常：${probe.stats}`);
assert(probe.steps >= 1, `归一化步骤未渲染：${probe.steps}`);
assert(probe.hits.length > 0, '证据区为空');
assert(probe.imgs >= 1, `图像案例未渲染：${probe.imgs}`);

console.log('score =', probe.score, '| badge =', probe.badge);
console.log('指标卡 =', probe.stats, '| 步骤行 =', probe.steps, '| 图像案例 =', probe.imgs);
console.log('证据 =', probe.hits.slice(0, 60));
console.log('[ok] demo 渲染正常，单文件自包含成立');

```

`tools/parity_node.js`:

```js
// Node 侧对拍脚本：读入文本列表 + 配置，输出 raven-lite.js 的打分
const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");

const code = fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, "..", "demo", "raven-lite.js"), "utf8");
// raven-lite.js 把自身挂到 window 或 globalThis 上
eval(code);

const payload = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(process.argv[2], "utf8"));
RavenLite.init(payload.cfg);

const out = payload.texts.map(t => {
  const r = RavenLite.score(t);
  return { p: r.p, p_rule: r.p_rule, norm: r.norm };
});
fs.writeFileSync(process.argv[3], JSON.stringify(out));
console.log(`[node] scored ${out.length} texts`);
```

`tools/parity_test.py`:

```py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""前后端一致性对拍（parity test）。

为什么要有这个：demo 声称"页面里跑的就是同一套模型"。这句话如果不验证，
它就是一个营销话术。这个脚本把 200+ 条真实样本同时喂给
  (a) Python 引擎（raven/），(b) 浏览器内核（demo/raven-lite.js，用 Node 跑），
逐条比概率，输出最大绝对误差。

跑法：python tools/parity_test.py
"""
import json
import os
import random
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys

import numpy as np

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from raven.pipeline import ART, TextEngine, read_jsonl
from raven.text_model import HAND_DIM, LogisticModel


def python_engine(bundle):
    m = LogisticModel(bundle["dim"] + HAND_DIM)
    m.w = np.array(bundle["text_model"]["w"], dtype=np.float32)
    m.b = bundle["text_model"]["b"]
    return TextEngine(m, threshold=bundle["threshold"])


def collect_texts(n=220):
    rng = random.Random(7)
    texts = []
    for split in ("test_clean", "test_adv", "test_pervasive", "test_holdout"):
        rows = read_jsonl(os.path.join(ART, "dataset", f"text_{split}.jsonl"))
        texts += [r["text"] for r in rng.sample(rows, min(55, len(rows)))]
    # 再加几条人为构造的刁钻输入
    texts += [
        "出·原·神·外·挂·加·薇·信",
        "外\u200b挂 自瞄 包退",
        "ＣＳ２ 出号 走平台",
        "今天看到有人卖外挂，举报了",
        "求推荐新手角色",
    ]
    return texts


def main():
    with open(os.path.join(ART, "models", "engine.json"), encoding="utf-8") as f:
        bundle = json.load(f)
    eng = python_engine(bundle)
    texts = collect_texts()

    payload = {"cfg": bundle, "texts": texts}
    tmp_in = os.path.join(ART, "parity_in.json")
    tmp_out = os.path.join(ART, "parity_out.json")
    with open(tmp_in, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(payload, f, ensure_ascii=False)

    node = shutil.which("node")
    if node is None:
        # 常见兜底位置（不同机器/不同安装方式），仍然找不到就跳过对拍
        for cand in (
            r"C:\Program Files\nodejs\node.exe",
            "/usr/local/bin/node",
            "/usr/bin/node",
        ):
            if os.path.exists(cand):
                node = cand
                break
    if node is None:
        print("未找到 node，跳过对拍（不影响其余产物，但 demo 的一致性未被验证）")
        for p in (tmp_in, tmp_out):
            if os.path.exists(p):
                os.remove(p)
        return
    r = subprocess.run([node, os.path.join(ROOT, "tools", "parity_node.js"), tmp_in, tmp_out],
                       capture_output=True, text=True)
    print(r.stdout.strip() or r.stderr.strip())
    if r.returncode != 0:
        print("Node 执行失败，跳过对拍")
        return

    with open(tmp_out, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        js = json.load(f)

    diffs, ndiff_norm, worst = [], 0, None
    for t, j in zip(texts, js):
        py = eng.score(t, explain=False)
        d = abs(py["p"] - j["p"])
        diffs.append(d)
        if py["norm"] != j["norm"]:
            ndiff_norm += 1
            if worst is None:
                worst = (t, py["norm"], j["norm"])
    diffs = np.array(diffs)
    print(f"样本数 {len(texts)} ｜ 最大概率差 {diffs.max():.6f} ｜ 平均 {diffs.mean():.8f}")
    print(f"归一化结果不一致 {ndiff_norm} 条")
    if worst:
        print("  首条差异：", worst)
    ok = diffs.max() < 5e-3 and ndiff_norm == 0
    print("对拍结论：" + ("通过（前端与后端是同一套判定）" if ok else "未通过，需要修"))
    os.remove(tmp_in)
    os.remove(tmp_out)
    return ok


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```
